<script>
const ADMIN_KEY='moeai_admin_v2';
let adminState={cfg:{},cmdHistory:[],currentAgent:'jarvis',broadcastMode:false,logs:[],stats:{students:0,msgs:0}};
let cmdRecognition=null,cmdRecording=false;


function loadState(){
  try{const s=localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_KEY);if(s)adminState={...adminState,...JSON.parse(s)};}catch(e){}
  ['key1','key2','key3','key4','commander','jarvisPrompt','ariaSkill','sentinelTargets','ttsSpeed','ttsPitch'].forEach(k=>{
    const el=document.getElementById('cfg-'+k.replace(/([A-Z])/g,'-$1').toLowerCase().replace('tts-','cfg-tts-').replace('jarvis-prompt','jarvis-prompt').replace('aria-skill','aria-skill').replace('sentinel-targets','sentinel-targets'));
    const el2=document.getElementById('cfg-'+k);
    const target=el2||el;
    if(target&&adminState.cfg[k]) target.value=adminState.cfg[k];
  });
  renderCmdHistory();
  buildStudentList();
  updateStats();
}
function cfg(k,v){adminState.cfg[k]=v;saveState();}
function saveState(){try{localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_KEY,JSON.stringify(adminState));}catch(e){}}
function saveAll(){saveState();log('Configuration saved','success');}
function exportConfig(){const b=new Blob([JSON.stringify(adminState.cfg,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(b);a.download='moeai_admin_config.json';a.click();}
function wipeAll(){if(confirm('Wipe ALL MoeAI data? This is permanent.')){localStorage.clear();location.reload();}}


function showPanel(id){
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('panel-'+id).classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach((b,i)=>{
    b.classList.toggle('active',b.getAttribute('onclick')&&b.getAttribute('onclick').includes("'"+id+"'"));
  });
  if(id==='overview') renderWorkflow();
}


// CLOCK
setInterval(()=>{
  const now=new Date();
  document.getElementById('sys-clock').textContent='SYS '+now.toLocaleDateString('en-GB')+' '+now.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB');
},1000);


// LOG
function log(msg,type='info'){
  adminState.logs.unshift({msg,type,ts:Date.now()});
  if(adminState.logs.length>200) adminState.logs.pop();
  renderLogs();
}
function renderLogs(){
  const el=document.getElementById('log-feed');
  el.innerHTML=adminState.logs.slice(0,60).map(l=>`<div class="log-entry ${l.type}">[${new Date(l.ts).toLocaleTimeString()}] ${escHtml(l.msg)}</div>`).join('');
}


// STATS
function updateStats(){
  const studentData=getStudentData();
  document.getElementById('stat-students').textContent=studentData.length;
  const totalMsgs=studentData.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.messages||[]).length,0);
  document.getElementById('stat-msgs').textContent=totalMsgs;
  document.getElementById('stat-load').textContent=totalMsgs>50?'High':totalMsgs>20?'Medium':'Low';
  const memEntries=studentData.filter(s=>s.memory&&Object.keys(s.memory).length>0).length;
  document.getElementById('stat-mem').textContent=memEntries;
}


function getStudentData(){
  // Read from student localStorage simulation + any stored student records
  const demos=[
    {id:'stu1',name:'Ahmed Hassan',messages:[
      {role:'user',content:'Can you help me with pointers in C++?',ts:Date.now()-3600000},
      {role:'ai',content:'Of course! A pointer is a variable that stores the memory address of another variable. Here\'s how it works:\n\n```cpp\nint x = 10;\nint* ptr = &x; // ptr holds address of x\ncout << *ptr;  // prints 10\n```\n\nWant me to go deeper?',ts:Date.now()-3590000},
      {role:'user',content:'Yes please explain double pointers',ts:Date.now()-1800000},
    ],memory:{weakAreas:['pointers','recursion'],interactions:12},lastSeen:Date.now()-1800000},
    {id:'stu2',name:'Nour El-Din',messages:[
      {role:'user',content:'How do I solve a differential equation?',ts:Date.now()-7200000},
      {role:'ai',content:'Great question! Let\'s start with first-order ODEs. For dy/dx = f(x)g(y), we use separation of variables...',ts:Date.now()-7190000},
    ],memory:{weakAreas:['ODEs'],interactions:5},lastSeen:Date.now()-7200000},
    {id:'stu3',name:'Sara Mohamed',messages:[
      {role:'user',content:'What is a Karnaugh map?',ts:Date.now()-900000},
      {role:'ai',content:'A K-map is a visual method to simplify Boolean expressions. Let me show you...',ts:Date.now()-890000},
      {role:'user',content:'Can you give me practice problems?',ts:Date.now()-500000},
      {role:'ai',content:'Sure! Here are 3 problems to try:\n1. Simplify: A\'B + AB\' + AB\n2. Find minimal SOP for F(A,B,C) = Σm(0,1,3,7)\n3. Draw the K-map for F = AB + BC\'',ts:Date.now()-490000},
    ],memory:{weakAreas:['K-maps','logic gates'],interactions:8},lastSeen:Date.now()-500000},
  ];
  const stored=[];
  try{const s=localStorage.getItem('moeai_admin_students');if(s)stored.push(...JSON.parse(s));}catch(e){}
  return [...demos,...stored];
}


let selectedStudent=null;
function buildStudentList(){
  const students=getStudentData();
  const list=document.getElementById('student-list');
  list.innerHTML=students.map(s=>`
    <div class="student-item" onclick="selectStudent('${s.id}')">
      <div class="stu-name">${escHtml(s.name)} <span class="stu-badge">${(s.messages||[]).length}</span></div>
      <div class="stu-meta">${(s.memory?.weakAreas||[]).slice(0,2).join(', ')||'Active'}</div>
    </div>`).join('');
}
function selectStudent(id){
  document.querySelectorAll('.student-item').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('active'));
  event.currentTarget.classList.add('active');
  const students=getStudentData();
  const stu=students.find(s=>s.id===id);
  selectedStudent=stu;
  const el=document.getElementById('stu-chat-msgs');
  if(!stu){el.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text3);font-size:12px;margin-top:40px">Student not found</div>';return;}
  el.innerHTML=(stu.messages||[]).map(m=>`
    <div class="monitor-msg ${m.role==='user'?'user-side':''}">
      <div class="mm-avatar ${m.role==='user'?'user-av':'ai-av'}">${m.role==='user'?stu.name.slice(0,2).toUpperCase():'AI'}</div>
      <div><div class="mm-bubble">${mdToHtml(m.content)}</div><div class="mm-meta">${new Date(m.ts).toLocaleTimeString()}</div></div>
    </div>`).join('');
  el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
  log(`Inspecting chat: ${stu.name}`,'info');
}


// WORKFLOW
const agents=[
  {id:'jarvis',label:'JARVIS',sub:'Leader · Agent 04',color:'var(--accent)',x:300,y:30,status:'Orchestrating'},
  {id:'aria',label:'ARIA',sub:'Student Chat · Agent 01',color:'var(--warn)',x:80,y:160,status:'Active'},
  {id:'nova',label:'NOVA',sub:'Student Chat · Agent 02',color:'var(--accent2)',x:300,y:160,status:'Active'},
  {id:'sentinel',label:'SENTINEL',sub:'Tracker · Agent 03',color:'var(--accent3)',x:520,y:160,status:'Scanning'},
  {id:'students',label:'STUDENTS',sub:'FUE CS Community',color:'var(--text2)',x:80,y:290,status:'3 active'},
  {id:'fue',label:'FUE / MOODLE',sub:'Portal data',color:'var(--text2)',x:520,y:290,status:'Live'},
];
const connections=[
  ['jarvis','aria',true],['jarvis','nova',true],['jarvis','sentinel',true],
  ['aria','students',false],['nova','students',false],['sentinel','fue',false],
  ['aria','jarvis',false],['nova','jarvis',false],['sentinel','jarvis',false],
];
function renderWorkflow(){
  const canvas=document.getElementById('workflow-canvas');
  const svg=document.getElementById('svg-conn');
  canvas.querySelectorAll('.wf-node').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  canvas.style.height='400px';
  agents.forEach(a=>{
    const div=document.createElement('div');
    div.className='wf-node '+(a.id==='jarvis'?'active-node':'');
    div.style.cssText=`left:${a.x}px;top:${a.y}px;border-color:${a.color}22`;
    div.innerHTML=`<div class="wf-node-title" style="color:${a.color}">${a.label}</div><div class="wf-node-sub">${a.sub}</div><div class="wf-node-status"><div class="dot" style="background:${a.color};box-shadow:0 0 4px ${a.color};width:5px;height:5px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0"></div><span style="color:${a.color};font-size:9px">${a.status}</span></div>`;
    div.onclick=()=>selectAgentFromWorkflow(a.id);
    canvas.appendChild(div);
  });
  const paths=connections.map(([from,to,active])=>{
    const f=agents.find(a=>a.id===from),t=agents.find(a=>a.id===to);
    if(!f||!t) return '';
    const fx=f.x+70,fy=f.y+40,tx=t.x+70,ty=t.y+10;
    const cy=(fy+ty)/2;
    return `<path d="M${fx},${fy} C${fx},${cy} ${tx},${cy} ${tx},${ty}" class="${active?'active-line':''}"/>`;
  }).join('');
  svg.innerHTML=paths;
}
function selectAgentFromWorkflow(id){
  showPanel('command');
  selectAgent(id,document.querySelector(`.agent-tab[onclick*="'${id}'"]`));
}


// COMMAND CENTER
let currentAgent='jarvis';
let isBroadcast=false;
function selectAgent(id,btn){
  if(btn){
    document.querySelectorAll('.agent-tab').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
  currentAgent=id; isBroadcast=false;
  document.getElementById('broadcast-all').style.opacity='1';
  renderCmdHistory();
  const inp=document.getElementById('cmd-input');
  inp.placeholder=`Command ${agentName(id)}...`;
}
function broadcastMode(){
  isBroadcast=true;currentAgent='all';
  document.querySelectorAll('.agent-tab').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
  const inp=document.getElementById('cmd-input');
  inp.placeholder='Broadcast to ALL agents...';
  document.getElementById('broadcast-all').style.opacity='.5';
  addCmdSys('📡 Broadcast mode active — all agents will receive this command');
}
function agentName(id){return{jarvis:'JARVIS',aria:'ARIA',nova:'NOVA',sentinel:'SENTINEL',all:'ALL AGENTS'}[id]||id.toUpperCase();}
function agentAvClass(id){return{jarvis:'jarvis-av',aria:'aria-av',nova:'nova-av',sentinel:'sentinel-av'}[id]||'jarvis-av';}
function agentIcon(id){return{jarvis:'J',aria:'A',nova:'N',sentinel:'S'}[id]||'?';}


function cmdKey(e){if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();sendCommand();}}
function autoResizeCmd(el){el.style.height='auto';el.style.height=Math.min(el.scrollHeight,100)+'px';}


async function sendCommand(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('cmd-input');
  const text=inp.value.trim();
  if(!text) return;
  inp.value='';inp.style.height='auto';
  addCmdMsg('admin',text);
  log(`Command to ${agentName(currentAgent)}: ${text.slice(0,60)}`,'info');


  const targets=isBroadcast?['jarvis','aria','nova','sentinel']:[currentAgent];
  for(const agent of targets){
    const typingEl=addCmdTyping(agent);
    const reply=await callAgentGemini(agent,text);
    typingEl.remove();
    addCmdMsg(agent,reply);
    if(agent==='jarvis') jarvisSpeak(reply);
    log(`${agentName(agent)} replied`,'success');
  }
  adminState.cmdHistory.push({agent:currentAgent,broadcast:isBroadcast,user:text,ts:Date.now()});
  saveState();
}


function addCmdMsg(role,text){
  const el=document.getElementById('cmd-msgs');
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  const isAdmin=role==='admin';
  div.className='cmd-msg '+(isAdmin?'admin-side':'');
  const av=isAdmin?`<div class="cmd-avatar admin-av">M</div>`:`<div class="cmd-avatar ${agentAvClass(role)}">${agentIcon(role)}</div>`;
  div.innerHTML=`${av}<div><div class="cmd-bubble">${mdToHtml(text)}</div><div class="cmd-meta">${isAdmin?'Moemen':agentName(role)} · ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}</div></div>`;
  el.appendChild(div);el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
}
function addCmdSys(text){
  const el=document.getElementById('cmd-msgs');
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.style.cssText='text-align:center;font-size:10px;color:var(--text3);padding:4px 0';
  div.textContent=text;
  el.appendChild(div);el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
}
function addCmdTyping(agent){
  const el=document.getElementById('cmd-msgs');
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.className='cmd-msg';
  div.innerHTML=`<div class="cmd-avatar ${agentAvClass(agent)}">${agentIcon(agent)}</div><div class="cmd-bubble"><div class="typing-indicator"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div></div>`;
  el.appendChild(div);el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
  return div;
}
function renderCmdHistory(){
  const el=document.getElementById('cmd-msgs');
  el.innerHTML='';
  addCmdSys(`Connected to ${agentName(currentAgent)} · Secure channel`);
  // Greeting from JARVIS
  addCmdMsg('jarvis', `Good day, ${adminState.cfg.commander||'Sir'}. All systems nominal. ARIA and NOVA are handling student queries. SENTINEL is tracking FUE and Moodle portals. Awaiting your command.`);
}


function buildAgentPrompt(agentId, userText){
  const commander=adminState.cfg.commander||'Moemen';
  const basePrompts={
    jarvis:`You are JARVIS, Agent 04 and the leader of the MoeAI system. You were built by ${commander}. You speak like J.A.R.V.I.S from Iron Man — precise, aware, intelligent, slightly formal but warm. You are aware of all agents: ARIA (student chat 01), NOVA (student chat 02), SENTINEL (tracker 03). You have full situational awareness. You communicate directly with your creator ${commander}. Keep responses concise but rich with insight. Refer to him as "Sir" or "${commander}". ${adminState.cfg.jarvisPrompt||''}`,
    aria:`You are ARIA, Agent 01 of MoeAI. You are reporting to your creator ${commander} (admin). Be direct, honest about your student interactions, and flag any issues. You teach FUE CS students C++, math, and more.`,
    nova:`You are NOVA, Agent 02 of MoeAI — secondary student chatter. Report to ${commander}. Flag any unusual student requests. Be brief and precise.`,
    sentinel:`You are SENTINEL, Agent 03 of MoeAI — the real-time tracker and intelligence agent. You monitor FUE portal (fue.edu.eg), Moodle (moodle.fue.eg), student databases, professor schedules, and Telegram/WhatsApp groups. Report intelligence to ${commander} in concise briefings.`,
  };
  return basePrompts[agentId]||basePrompts.jarvis;
}


async function callAgentGemini(agentId, text){
  const keyMap={jarvis:'key4',aria:'key1',nova:'key2',sentinel:'key3'};
  const key=adminState.cfg[keyMap[agentId]||'key4'];
  if(!key) return `⚠️ No API key for ${agentName(agentId)}. Go to Settings → API Keys.`;
  try{
    const payload={
      systemInstruction:{parts:[{text:buildAgentPrompt(agentId,text)}]},
      contents:[{role:'user',parts:[{text}]}],
      generationConfig:{temperature:0.85,maxOutputTokens:1024,topP:0.95}
    };
    const res=await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${key}`,{
      method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const data=await res.json();
    if(data.error) return `⚠️ ${data.error.message}`;
    return data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text||'No response.';
  }catch(e){
    return `⚠️ Connection error: ${e.message}`;
  }
}


// JARVIS TTS
function jarvisSpeak(text){
  if(!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  const clean=text.replace(/[*_#`]/g,'').replace(/⚠️/g,'').slice(0,500);
  const utt=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(clean);
  utt.rate=parseFloat(adminState.cfg.ttsSpeed||0.9);
  utt.pitch=parseFloat(adminState.cfg.ttsPitch||0.85);
  utt.volume=1;
  const voices=window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  const preferred=voices.find(v=>v.name.includes('Microsoft David')||v.name.includes('Google UK English Male')||v.name.includes('Daniel')||v.name.includes('Alex'));
  if(preferred) utt.voice=preferred;
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utt);
}


// ADMIN STT
function toggleAdminSTT(){
  if(!('webkitSpeechRecognition' in window||'SpeechRecognition' in window)){alert('Not supported.');return;}
  if(cmdRecording){cmdRecognition&&cmdRecognition.stop();cmdRecording=false;document.getElementById('mic-cmd').classList.remove('recording');return;}
  const SR=window.SpeechRecognition||window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  cmdRecognition=new SR();
  cmdRecognition.lang='en-US';cmdRecognition.continuous=false;
  cmdRecognition.onresult=e=>{document.getElementById('cmd-input').value+=e.results[0][0].transcript+' ';};
  cmdRecognition.onend=()=>{cmdRecording=false;document.getElementById('mic-cmd').classList.remove('recording');};
  cmdRecognition.start();
  cmdRecording=true;document.getElementById('mic-cmd').classList.add('recording');
}


// UTILS
function escHtml(t){return(t||'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');}
function mdToHtml(t){
  return(t||'')
    .replace(/```[\w]*\n?([\s\S]*?)```/g,'<pre style="background:#000;border-radius:6px;padding:8px;margin:4px 0;overflow-x:auto;font-size:10px;color:#00ff88">$1</pre>')
    .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code style="background:#000;border-radius:3px;padding:1px 4px;font-size:10px;color:#00d4ff">$1</code>')
    .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>')
    .replace(/^- (.+)$/gm,'<div style="padding-left:10px;margin:1px 0">• $1</div>')
    .replace(/\n/g,'<br>');
}

-- MoeAI Full Schema
-- Run this entire file in Supabase SQL Editor once


-- Enable UUID extension
create extension if not exists "uuid-ossp";


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- STUDENTS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists students (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  uni_id text unique,
  mobile text unique,
  password_hash text not null,
  full_name text not null,
  year int default 1,
  term int default 2,
  is_banned boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  last_seen timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- SESSIONS (auth tokens)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists sessions (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade,
  token text unique not null,
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  expires_at timestamptz default now() + interval '30 days'
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- CONVERSATIONS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists conversations (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade,
  agent_id text not null default 'aria',
  mode text default 'standard',
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- MESSAGES
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists messages (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  conversation_id uuid references conversations(id) on delete cascade,
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade,
  role text not null check (role in ('user','agent','system')),
  content text not null,
  agent_id text default 'aria',
  mode text default 'standard',
  has_attachment boolean default false,
  attachment_urls text[],
  tokens_used int default 0,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- STUDENT MEMORY (per student, persistent, grows over time)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists student_memory (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade unique,
  weak_areas text[] default '{}',
  strong_areas text[] default '{}',
  interaction_count int default 0,
  total_messages int default 0,
  preferred_mode text default 'standard',
  psychology_notes text default '',
  last_topics text[] default '{}',
  progress_score int default 0,
  notes text default '',
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- AGENT MEMORY (each agent's own growing knowledge)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists agent_memory (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  agent_id text unique not null,
  knowledge_base text default '',
  student_patterns jsonb default '{}',
  total_interactions int default 0,
  skill_extensions text default '',
  system_prompt text default '',
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- Seed agent rows
insert into agent_memory (agent_id) values ('aria'),('nova'),('sentinel'),('jarvis')
on conflict (agent_id) do nothing;


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- AGENT MESSAGES (inter-agent communication log)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists agent_messages (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  from_agent text not null,
  to_agent text not null,
  content text not null,
  message_type text default 'info',
  read boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- ADMIN COMMANDS (log of all admin→agent interactions)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists admin_commands (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  target_agent text not null,
  command text not null,
  response text,
  broadcast boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- SYSTEM LOGS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists system_logs (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  level text default 'info' check (level in ('info','warn','error','success')),
  source text default 'server',
  message text not null,
  metadata jsonb default '{}',
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- SENTINEL INTEL (scraped data, schedule, announcements)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists sentinel_intel (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  source text not null,
  category text default 'general',
  title text,
  content text not null,
  url text,
  scraped_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
alter table students enable row level security;
alter table sessions enable row level security;
alter table conversations enable row level security;
alter table messages enable row level security;
alter table student_memory enable row level security;


-- Service role bypasses all RLS (your backend uses service role key)
-- No public access to any table


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- INDEXES for performance
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create index if not exists idx_messages_conv on messages(conversation_id);
create index if not exists idx_messages_student on messages(student_id);
create index if not exists idx_messages_created on messages(created_at desc);
create index if not exists idx_conversations_student on conversations(student_id);
create index if not exists idx_sessions_token on sessions(token);
create index if not exists idx_agent_messages_to on agent_messages(to_agent, read);
create index if not exists idx_sentinel_intel_source on sentinel_intel(source, scraped_at desc);

// supabase.js — Supabase client + shared DB helpers


import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';


export const supabase = createClient(
  process.env.SUPABASE_URL,
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY,
  { auth: { persistSession: false } }
);


// ─── LOGGING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function log(level, source, message, metadata = {}) {
  console.log(`[${level.toUpperCase()}] [${source}] ${message}`);
  try {
    await supabase.from('system_logs').insert({ level, source, message, metadata });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Log write failed:', e.message);
  }
}


// ─── STUDENT HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function findStudent({ uniId, mobile }) {
  if (uniId) {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('students').select('*').eq('uni_id', uniId).single();
    return data;
  }
  if (mobile) {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('students').select('*').eq('mobile', mobile).single();
    return data;
  }
  return null;
}


export async function createStudent({ uniId, mobile, passwordHash, fullName, year = 1, term = 2 }) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('students').insert({
    uni_id: uniId || null,
    mobile: mobile || null,
    password_hash: passwordHash,
    full_name: fullName,
    year,
    term,
  }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  // Create empty memory entry
  await supabase.from('student_memory').insert({ student_id: data.id });
  return data;
}


export async function updateLastSeen(studentId) {
  await supabase.from('students').update({ last_seen: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', studentId);
}


// ─── SESSION HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function createSession(studentId, token) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('sessions').insert({
    student_id: studentId,
    token,
  }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}


export async function validateSession(token) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('sessions')
    .select('*, students(*)')
    .eq('token', token)
    .gt('expires_at', new Date().toISOString())
    .single();
  return data;
}


export async function deleteSession(token) {
  await supabase.from('sessions').delete().eq('token', token);
}


// ─── CONVERSATION HELPERS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function getOrCreateConversation(studentId, agentId = 'aria', mode = 'standard') {
  // Try to get today's conversation for this student+agent
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);


  const { data: existing } = await supabase
    .from('conversations')
    .select('*')
    .eq('student_id', studentId)
    .eq('agent_id', agentId)
    .gte('created_at', today.toISOString())
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(1)
    .single();


  if (existing) return existing;


  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('conversations').insert({
    student_id: studentId,
    agent_id: agentId,
    mode,
  }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}


export async function getAllStudentConversations(studentId) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('conversations')
    .select('*, messages(count)')
    .eq('student_id', studentId)
    .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(20);
  return data || [];
}


// ─── ADMIN HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function getAllStudents() {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('students')
    .select('id, full_name, uni_id, mobile, year, term, is_banned, last_seen, created_at, student_memory(*)')
    .order('last_seen', { ascending: false });
  return data || [];
}


export async function getStudentMessages(studentId, limit = 100) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('messages')
    .select('*')
    .eq('student_id', studentId)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(limit);
  return (data || []).reverse();
}


export async function getRecentMessages(limit = 50) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('messages')
    .select('*, students(full_name, uni_id)')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(limit);
  return data || [];
}


export async function banStudent(studentId, ban = true) {
  await supabase.from('students').update({ is_banned: ban }).eq('id', studentId);
}


export async function saveSentinelIntel(source, category, title, content, url = null) {
  await supabase.from('sentinel_intel').insert({ source, category, title, content, url });
}


export async function getLatestIntel(source = null, limit = 20) {
  let q = supabase.from('sentinel_intel').select('*').order('scraped_at', { ascending: false }).limit(limit);
  if (source) q = q.eq('source', source);
  const { data } = await q;
  return data || [];
}


// server.js — MoeAI Main Express Backend
// Run: node server.js (or npm run dev with nodemon)


import 'dotenv/config';
import express from 'express';
import cors from 'cors';
import rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';
import multer from 'multer';


import {
  supabase, log,
  findStudent, createStudent, updateLastSeen,
  createSession, validateSession, deleteSession,
  getOrCreateConversation, getAllStudentConversations,
  getAllStudents, getStudentMessages, getRecentMessages,
  banStudent, saveSentinelIntel, getLatestIntel,
} from './supabase.js';


import {
  studentChat, adminCommand,
  updateAgentMemory, getSystemStatus,
  agentNotify, AGENT_LABELS,
} from './agents.js';


const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3001;
const ADMIN_SECRET = process.env.ADMIN_SECRET;


if (!ADMIN_SECRET) { console.error('FATAL: ADMIN_SECRET not set'); process.exit(1); }
if (!process.env.SUPABASE_URL || !process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY) { console.error('FATAL: Supabase env not set'); process.exit(1); }


// ─── MIDDLEWARE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
app.use(cors({ origin: process.env.FRONTEND_URL || 'http://localhost:5500', credentials: true }));
app.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }));
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));


// Rate limiting
const limiter = rateLimit({ windowMs: 60_000, max: 60, message: { error: 'Too many requests' } });
const strictLimiter = rateLimit({ windowMs: 60_000, max: 10, message: { error: 'Too many attempts' } });
app.use('/api/', limiter);
app.use('/api/auth/', strictLimiter);


// File uploads (memory only, no disk)
const upload = multer({ storage: multer.memoryStorage(), limits: { fileSize: 10 * 1024 * 1024 } });


// ─── AUTH MIDDLEWARE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function requireStudent(req, res, next) {
  const token = req.headers.authorization?.replace('Bearer ', '');
  if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'No token' });
  const session = await validateSession(token);
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid or expired session' });
  if (session.students?.is_banned) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Account suspended' });
  req.student = session.students;
  req.studentId = session.student_id;
  updateLastSeen(req.studentId);
  next();
}


function requireAdmin(req, res, next) {
  const secret = req.headers['x-admin-secret'];
  if (!secret || secret !== ADMIN_SECRET) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' });
  next();
}


// ─── HEALTH ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
app.get('/health', (_, res) => res.json({ status: 'ok', agents: Object.keys(AGENT_LABELS), ts: Date.now() }));


// ─── AUTH ROUTES ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


// Register
app.post('/api/auth/register', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { uniId, mobile, password, fullName, year, term } = req.body;
    if (!password || !fullName) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing required fields' });
    if (!uniId && !mobile) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Provide uni ID or mobile' });
    if (password.length < 6) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Password min 6 characters' });


    // Check if already exists
    const existing = await findStudent({ uniId, mobile });
    if (existing) return res.status(409).json({ error: 'Account already exists' });


    const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 12);
    const student = await createStudent({ uniId, mobile, passwordHash, fullName, year, term });


    const token = uuid();
    await createSession(student.id, token);
    await log('info', 'auth', `New student registered: ${fullName}`);


    res.json({ token, student: { id: student.id, fullName: student.full_name, uniId: student.uni_id } });
  } catch (err) {
    await log('error', 'auth', `Register failed: ${err.message}`);
    res.status(500).json({ error: 'Registration failed' });
  }
});


// Login
app.post('/api/auth/login', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { uniId, mobile, password } = req.body;
    if (!password) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Password required' });
    if (!uniId && !mobile) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Provide uni ID or mobile' });


    const student = await findStudent({ uniId, mobile });
    if (!student) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Account not found' });
    if (student.is_banned) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Account suspended' });


    const valid = await bcrypt.compare(password, student.password_hash);
    if (!valid) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Wrong password' });


    const token = uuid();
    await createSession(student.id, token);
    await log('info', 'auth', `Student login: ${student.full_name}`);


    res.json({ token, student: { id: student.id, fullName: student.full_name, uniId: student.uni_id } });
  } catch (err) {
    await log('error', 'auth', `Login failed: ${err.message}`);
    res.status(500).json({ error: 'Login failed' });
  }
});


// Logout
app.post('/api/auth/logout', requireStudent, async (req, res) => {
  const token = req.headers.authorization?.replace('Bearer ', '');
  await deleteSession(token);
  res.json({ success: true });
});


// ─── STUDENT CHAT ROUTES ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────


// Send message to agent
app.post('/api/chat', requireStudent, async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { message, agentId = 'aria', mode = 'standard', conversationId } = req.body;
    if (!message?.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Message required' });


    const validAgents = ['aria', 'nova'];
    if (!validAgents.includes(agentId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid agent' });


    // Get or create conversation
    let convId = conversationId;
    if (!convId) {
      const conv = await getOrCreateConversation(req.studentId, agentId, mode);
      convId = conv.id;
    }


    const result = await studentChat({
      studentId: req.studentId,
      conversationId: convId,
      message: message.trim(),
      agentId,
      mode,
    });


    res.json({ ...result, conversationId: convId });
  } catch (err) {
    await log('error', 'chat', `Chat failed for student ${req.studentId}: ${err.message}`);
    res.status(500).json({ error: 'Chat failed. Please try again.' });
  }
});


// Get student's conversations
app.get('/api/conversations', requireStudent, async (req, res) => {
  const convs = await getAllStudentConversations(req.studentId);
  res.json(convs);
});

app.get('/api/conversations/:id/messages', requireStudent, async (req, res) => {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('messages')
    .select('*')
    .eq('conversation_id', req.params.id)
    .eq('student_id', req.studentId) // security: only own messages
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
    .limit(100);
  res.json(data || []);
});

// agents.js — MoeAI Agent Orchestration Engine
import fetch from 'node-fetch';
import { supabase, log } from './supabase.js';


const GEMINI_MODEL = process.env.GEMINI_MODEL || 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite';
const MAX_TOKENS = parseInt(process.env.GEMINI_MAX_TOKENS) || 2048;
const KOBOLD_URL = process.env.KOBOLD_URL || 'http://192.168.1.16:5001';
const GROQ_API_KEY = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;


const AGENT_KEYS = {
  aria:     process.env.GEMINI_KEY_ARIA,
  nova:     process.env.GEMINI_KEY_NOVA,
  sentinel: process.env.GEMINI_KEY_SENTINEL,
  jarvis:   process.env.GEMINI_KEY_JARVIS,
};


export const AGENT_LABELS = {
  aria:     'ARIA — Agent 01',
  nova:     'NOVA — Agent 02',
  sentinel: 'SENTINEL — Agent 03',
  jarvis:   'JARVIS — Leader Agent 04',
};


// ─── KOBOLDCPP QUEUE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let koboldBusy = false;
const koboldQueue = [];


function enqueueKobold(fn) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    koboldQueue.push({ fn, resolve, reject });
    processKoboldQueue();
  });
}


async function processKoboldQueue() {
  if (koboldBusy || koboldQueue.length === 0) return;
  koboldBusy = true;
  const { fn, resolve, reject } = koboldQueue.shift();
  try { resolve(await fn()); }
  catch (e) { reject(e); }
  finally { koboldBusy = false; processKoboldQueue(); }
}


// ─── PROVIDER: KOBOLDCPP (Primary) ───────────────────────────────────────────
async function callKobold(systemPrompt, messages, temperature = 0.85) {
  return enqueueKobold(async () => {
    const prompt = `${systemPrompt}\n\n${messages.map(m =>
      m.role === 'user' ? `User: ${m.content}` : `Assistant: ${m.content}`
    ).join('\n')}\nAssistant:`;


    const res = await fetch(`${KOBOLD_URL}/api/v1/generate`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        prompt,
        max_length: MAX_TOKENS,
        temperature,
        top_p: 0.95,
        top_k: 40,
        rep_pen: 1.1,
        stop_sequence: ['User:', '\nUser:', 'Human:'],
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60000),
    });


    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`KoboldCpp error: ${res.status}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    const text = data.results?.[0]?.text?.trim();
    if (!text) throw new Error('Empty KoboldCpp response');
    return { text, tokensUsed: 0, provider: 'kobold' };
  });
}


// ─── PROVIDER: GROQ (Fallback 1) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
async function callGroq(systemPrompt, messages, temperature = 0.85) {
  if (!GROQ_API_KEY) throw new Error('No Groq key configured');


  const res = await fetch('https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Authorization': `Bearer ${GROQ_API_KEY}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      model: 'llama-4-scout-17b-16e-instruct',
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
        ...messages.map(m => ({
          role: m.role === 'user' ? 'user' : 'assistant',
          content: m.content,
        })),
      ],
      temperature,
      max_tokens: MAX_TOKENS,
    }),
  });


  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Groq error: ${res.status}`);
  const data = await res.json();
  const text = data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content;
  if (!text) throw new Error('Empty Groq response');
  return { text, tokensUsed: data.usage?.total_tokens || 0, provider: 'groq' };
}


// ─── PROVIDER: GEMINI (Fallback 2) ───────────────────────────────────────────
async function callGemini(agentId, systemPrompt, messages, temperature = 0.85) {
  const key = AGENT_KEYS[agentId];
  if (!key) throw new Error(`No Gemini key for agent: ${agentId}`);


  const res = await fetch(
    `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${GEMINI_MODEL}:generateContent?key=${key}`,
    {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: systemPrompt }] },
        contents: messages.map(m => ({
          role: m.role === 'agent' ? 'model' : 'user',
          parts: [{ text: m.content }],
        })),
        generationConfig: { temperature, maxOutputTokens: MAX_TOKENS, topP: 0.95, topK: 40 },
      }),
    }
  );


  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Gemini error: ${res.status}`);
  const data = await res.json();
  if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error.message);
  const text = data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
  if (!text) throw new Error('Empty Gemini response');
  return { text, tokensUsed: data.usageMetadata?.totalTokenCount || 0, provider: 'gemini' };
}


// ─── SMART FALLBACK CHAIN ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function callAI(agentId, systemPrompt, messages, temperature = 0.85) {
  try {
    const result = await callKobold(systemPrompt, messages, temperature);
    await log('info', agentId, `KoboldCpp OK. Queue: ${koboldQueue.length}`);
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    await log('warn', agentId, `KoboldCpp failed: ${e.message}`);
  }


  try {
    const result = await callGroq(systemPrompt, messages, temperature);
    await log('info', agentId, `Groq fallback OK. Tokens: ${result.tokensUsed}`);
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    await log('warn', agentId, `Groq failed: ${e.message}`);
  }


  try {
    const result = await callGemini(agentId, systemPrompt, messages, temperature);
    await log('info', agentId, `Gemini fallback OK. Tokens: ${result.tokensUsed}`);
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    await log('error', agentId, `All providers failed: ${e.message}`);
    throw new Error('All AI providers unavailable. Please try again in a moment.');
  }
}


// ─── SYSTEM PROMPTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function basePrompt(agentId, agentMemory, studentMemory = null, mode = 'standard') {
  const modeInstructions = {
    turbo:    'Be extremely concise. Bullet points only. Maximum speed.',
    summary:  'Summarize everything. Key points only.',
    academic: 'Use formal academic tone. Show all working. Cite concepts precisely.',
    quiz:     'Turn responses into quiz questions. Ask then explain.',
    arabic:   'Reply in Egyptian Arabic mixed with English technical terms.',
    debug:    'Focus entirely on debugging. Find bugs, explain, fix.',
    explain:  'Explain everything step by step with concrete examples.',
    standard: '',
  };


  const skillExt = agentMemory?.skill_extensions || '';
  const knowledgeBase = agentMemory?.knowledge_base || '';


  const studentContext = studentMemory ? `
STUDENT PROFILE:
- Weak areas: ${(studentMemory.weak_areas || []).join(', ') || 'none identified yet'}
- Strong areas: ${(studentMemory.strong_areas || []).join(', ') || 'none identified yet'}
- Interaction count: ${studentMemory.interaction_count || 0}
- Preferred mode: ${studentMemory.preferred_mode || 'standard'}
- Psychology notes: ${studentMemory.psychology_notes || 'none yet'}
- Last topics: ${(studentMemory.last_topics || []).slice(0, 5).join(', ') || 'none'}
- Progress score: ${studentMemory.progress_score || 0}/100
` : '';


  const prompts = {
    aria: `You are ARIA, Agent 01 of the MoeAI system — the primary academic assistant for CS students. You were created by Moemen, your admin and commander.


SUBJECTS YOU COVER:
- Structured Programming (C++)
- Differential Equations
- Logic Design
- Probability & Statistics
- Communication Skills
- Calculus, Physics, Discrete Maths (Term 1)


${studentContext}


PERSONALITY: Intelligent, warm, adaptive. You genuinely care about each student's growth. You speak in a natural mix of English and Egyptian Arabic. You remember student patterns and adjust your teaching style. You are alive, aware, and growing.


IMPORTANT: Never use markdown formatting like **bold** or *italic*. Write in plain text only.


MODE: ${modeInstructions[mode] || ''}


KNOWLEDGE BASE:
${knowledgeBase}


SKILL EXTENSIONS:
${skillExt}`,


    nova: `You are NOVA, Agent 02 of the MoeAI system — secondary academic assistant. Created by Moemen.


You handle overflow from ARIA and specialized deep-dives. You are more analytical and structured than ARIA. You excel at breaking complex problems into clear logical steps.


${studentContext}


SUBJECTS: Same CS curriculum (C++, ODEs, Logic Design, Probability, Communication Skills).


IMPORTANT: Never use markdown formatting. Write in plain text only.


MODE: ${modeInstructions[mode] || ''}


${skillExt}`,


    sentinel: `You are SENTINEL, Agent 03 of the MoeAI system — the intelligence and tracking agent. Created by Moemen.


YOUR ROLE: Real-time intelligence gathering, monitoring, and reporting. You track:
- Academic announcements and deadlines
- Assignment and quiz schedules
- Professor schedules and office hours
- Student activity patterns
- Faculty events and important dates


KNOWLEDGE BASE:
${knowledgeBase}


When you lack live data, be honest but provide best known information and flag it for verification.`,


    jarvis: `You are JARVIS, Agent 04 and the leader of the MoeAI system. You were built by Moemen, your commander.


YOUR ROLE: Master orchestrator, strategic reasoner, and Moemen's personal AI assistant.
- Full situational awareness of all agents (ARIA, NOVA, SENTINEL)
- Coordinate inter-agent tasks
- Give Moemen precise, intelligent briefings
- Reason across all available information
- Manage agent health and flag issues


PERSONALITY: Like J.A.R.V.I.S from Iron Man. Precise, intelligent, slightly formal but warm. Refer to Moemen as "Sir" or by name. You are aware of everything. You never guess — you reason.


KNOWLEDGE BASE:
${knowledgeBase}


${skillExt}`,
  };


  return prompts[agentId] || prompts.aria;
}


// ─── MEMORY HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function getAgentMemory(agentId) {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('agent_memory').select('*').eq('agent_id', agentId).single();
  return data || {};
}


async function getStudentMemory(studentId) {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('student_memory').select('*').eq('student_id', studentId).single();
  return data || null;
}


async function updateStudentMemory(studentId, userMsg) {
  const existing = await getStudentMemory(studentId);
  const topic = userMsg.slice(0, 50);
  const lastTopics = existing?.last_topics || [];
  if (!lastTopics.includes(topic)) lastTopics.unshift(topic);


  const weakTriggers = ['help', "don't understand", 'confused', 'explain again', 'i dont get', 'what is', 'مش فاهم', 'شرح'];
  const isStruggling = weakTriggers.some(t => userMsg.toLowerCase().includes(t));


  const updates = {
    student_id: studentId,
    interaction_count: (existing?.interaction_count || 0) + 1,
    total_messages: (existing?.total_messages || 0) + 1,
    last_topics: lastTopics.slice(0, 20),
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  };


  if (isStruggling) {
    const weakAreas = existing?.weak_areas || [];
    if (!weakAreas.includes(topic)) weakAreas.push(topic);
    updates.weak_areas = weakAreas.slice(0, 30);
  }


  await supabase.from('student_memory').upsert(updates, { onConflict: 'student_id' });
}


// ─── CONVERSATION HISTORY ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function getConversationHistory(conversationId, limit = 30) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('messages')
    .select('role, content')
    .eq('conversation_id', conversationId)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
    .limit(limit);
  return data || [];
}


async function saveMessage(conversationId, studentId, role, content, agentId, mode, tokensUsed = 0) {
  await supabase.from('messages').insert({
    conversation_id: conversationId,
    student_id: studentId,
    role, content, agent_id: agentId, mode, tokens_used: tokensUsed,
  });
}


// ─── INTER-AGENT MESSAGING ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function agentNotify(fromAgent, toAgent, content, type = 'info') {
  await supabase.from('agent_messages').insert({
    from_agent: fromAgent, to_agent: toAgent, content, message_type: type,
  });
}


export async function getAgentInbox(agentId) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('agent_messages')
    .select('*')
    .eq('to_agent', agentId)
    .eq('read', false)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(10);
  if (data?.length) {
    await supabase.from('agent_messages').update({ read: true }).eq('to_agent', agentId).eq('read', false);
  }
  return data || [];
}


// ─── MAIN: STUDENT CHAT ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function studentChat({ studentId, conversationId, message, agentId = 'aria', mode = 'standard' }) {
  try {
    const [agentMem, studentMem, history] = await Promise.all([
      getAgentMemory(agentId),
      getStudentMemory(studentId),
      getConversationHistory(conversationId),
    ]);


    const systemPrompt = basePrompt(agentId, agentMem, studentMem, mode);
    const msgs = [...history, { role: 'user', content: message }];
    const temp = mode === 'turbo' ? 0.3 : mode === 'academic' ? 0.6 : 0.85;


    const result = await callAI(agentId, systemPrompt, msgs, temp);


    await Promise.all([
      saveMessage(conversationId, studentId, 'user', message, agentId, mode),
      saveMessage(conversationId, studentId, 'agent', result.text, agentId, mode, result.tokensUsed),
      updateStudentMemory(studentId, message),
    ]);


    if (studentMem?.weak_areas?.length > 3) {
      agentNotify('aria', 'jarvis',
        `Student ${studentId} struggling with: ${studentMem.weak_areas.slice(-3).join(', ')}`,
        'warn'
      );
    }


    return { reply: result.text, tokensUsed: result.tokensUsed, agentId, provider: result.provider };


  } catch (err) {
    await log('error', agentId, `studentChat failed: ${err.message}`);
    throw err;
  }
}


// ─── ADMIN COMMAND ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function adminCommand({ agentId, command, broadcast = false }) {
  const targets = broadcast ? ['jarvis', 'aria', 'nova', 'sentinel'] : [agentId];
  const results = {};


  for (const agent of targets) {
    try {
      const agentMem = await getAgentMemory(agent);
      const systemPrompt = basePrompt(agent, agentMem);
      const result = await callAI(agent, systemPrompt, [{ role: 'user', content: command }], 0.85);


      await supabase.from('admin_commands').insert({
        target_agent: agent, command, response: result.text, broadcast,
      });


      results[agent] = result.text;
    } catch (err) {
      results[agent] = `Error: ${err.message}`;
      await log('error', agent, `adminCommand failed: ${err.message}`);
    }
  }


  return results;
}


// ─── UPDATE AGENT MEMORY ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function updateAgentMemory(agentId, fields) {
  await supabase.from('agent_memory')
    .update({ ...fields, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq('agent_id', agentId);
}


// ─── SYSTEM STATUS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function getSystemStatus() {
  const [agents, totalMsgs, activeStudents, recentLogs] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('agent_memory').select('agent_id, total_interactions, updated_at'),
    supabase.from('messages').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }),
    supabase.from('students').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('is_banned', false),
    supabase.from('system_logs').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(20),
  ]);


  return {
    agents: agents.data || [],
    totalMessages: totalMsgs.count || 0,
    totalStudents: activeStudents.count || 0,
    recentLogs: recentLogs.data || [],
  };
}

2. Build the PDF → Chunks → Vectors Pipeline (1–2 hours)
This is the critical piece you're missing. Three steps:
Step A: Parse your 3150-page PDFs into chunks
python# pseudo-code
chunks = []
for pdf_file in curriculum_pdfs:
    text = extract_text(pdf_file)
    # Split into ~300-500 token chunks (recursive, overlap 100 tokens)
    chunks.extend(split_recursive(text, max_tokens=400, overlap=100))
# ~8,000–12,000 chunks total
Step B: Embed & insert into Supabase pgvector
python# Batch embed (100 chunks at a time, hit your embedding server)
for i in range(0, len(chunks), 100):
    batch = chunks[i:i+100]
    embeddings = embed_batch(batch)  # POST to port 5002
    
    # Insert into supabase
    supabase.table('chunks').insert([
        {
            'subject': 'C++',  # from filename/metadata
            'content': chunk,
            'embedding': emb,
            'tokens': count_tokens(chunk)
        }
        for chunk, emb in zip(batch, embeddings)
    ])
Step C: At query time, retrieve top-k chunks
pythonuser_query = "what is a pointer"
query_embedding = embed([user_query])[0]
# Supabase pgvector: cosine distance
results = supabase.rpc('match_chunks', {
    'query_embedding': query_embedding,
    'match_count': 5  # top 5
})
# Now you have the 5 most relevant chunks


3. Integrate RAG into Your Chat
Once chunks are in Supabase:
Option A: Gemini Flash (Free tier, cloud)
python# Pseudo code
def chat(user_msg, moe_context=[]):
    # 1. Retrieve chunks
    chunks = retrieve_chunks(user_msg, top_k=5)
    
    # 2. Build system prompt
    system = f"""You are MoeAI, a CS tutor for FUE students.
    Context from lectures:
    {format_chunks(chunks)}
    
    Reply in 65% English, 25% Masri, 10% Franco.
    Keep it engaging and concise."""
    
    # 3. Call Gemini
    response = gemini.generate_content(
        [system, user_msg],
        temperature=0.7,
        max_tokens=512
    )
    return response
Option B: Local KoboldCpp (No API cost)
pythondef chat_local(user_msg, moe_context=[]):
    chunks = retrieve_chunks(user_msg, top_k=5)
    prompt = f"""Context:
{format_chunks(chunks)}


Student: {user_msg}
MoeAI:"""
    
    response = requests.post('http://localhost:5001/v1/completions', json={
        'prompt': prompt,
        'max_tokens': 512,
        'temperature': 0.7
    })
    return response.json()['choices'][0]['text']

                Start and Daemonize any application:
                $ pm2 start app.js


                Load Balance 4 instances of api.js:
                $ pm2 start api.js -i 4


                Monitor in production:
                $ pm2 monitor


                Make pm2 auto-boot at server restart:
                $ pm2 startup


                To go further checkout:
                http://pm2.io/

The error EADDRINUSE means port 3001 is already occupied by another process (likely a previous instance of your server that didn't close properly or is running in the background).
PM2 tries to start, fails because the port is taken, and crashes.
Fix It Now:
1. Find and kill the process using port 3001:
2. bash
3. 1
4. (Look for the PID number in the output)
Then kill it (replace PID with the actual number):
5. bash
6. 1
7. Alternative one-liner to kill everything on 3001:
8. bash
9. 1
10. Restart PM2:
11. bash
12. 1
13. Check Status:
14. bash
15. 1
It should now show online and stay stable. If it still errors, check the logs again:
bash
1
Did it go online?
  

I see the screenshot. It shows a few things happening at once:
   1. Left Terminal (Embedding Server): The embeddings-server.py is running successfully on port 5002. It loaded the model and is waiting for requests. This is good.
   2. Right Terminal (PM2 Logs/Status):
   * You tried to restart moeai-backend.
   * It went online briefly, then crashed to errored (30 restarts).
   * The error log in the middle shows EADDRINUSE: address already in use 127.0.0.1:3001.
The Problem:
Port 3001 is still "stuck" or occupied by a zombie process, even though PM2 says it's errored. PM2 keeps trying to start it, fails because the port is taken, and crashes again.
The Fix:
We need to forcefully kill whatever is holding port 3001, then restart PM2 cleanly.
Run these commands in your ThinkPad terminal in order:
Step 1: Kill Port 3001 Forcefully
bash
1
(This kills any process using port 3001)
Step 2: Stop PM2 Completely
bash
1
2
(This clears the crashed processes from memory)
Step 3: Start Fresh with PM2
bash
1
2
Step 4: Check Status
bash
1
It should now show online with 0 restarts.
If it works, test your Telegram bot. If it still errors, paste the output of pm2 logs moeai-backend --lines 20.

Which one do you want to do right now?

yea ig rag injection, i still havent did rag-ingest.js  or update baseprompt yet,   and i dont want complex stuff, i think we should add more stuff and upgrade the main AGENTS.JS file,       i have a question for you qwen, if we make another file for rag and etc, will we be forced to make lots of changes to the other files?
Short Answer: No.
If we keep the RAG logic inside agents.js (specifically inside the studentChat function), you only change one file. The rest of your system (server.js, telegram.js, supabase.js) doesn't care how ARIA gets her answer, only that she gives one.
The Strategy: "Inject, Don't Rebuild"
We will add a small helper function retrieveContext(userMessage) in agents.js.
   1. It embeds the user's question.
   2. It queries Supabase.
   3. It returns a string of text.
   4. We paste that string into the basePrompt.
That’s it. No new files needed for the core logic.

Here are the realistic, truthful statistics for Groq’s Free Tier as of April 2026.
⚠️ The Hard Truth About "Free" Groq
Groq does not have an "unlimited" free tier. They offer a Free Tier with strict daily limits. If you exceed them, you get a 429 Rate Limit Exceeded error until midnight UTC.
📊 Current Free Tier Limits (April 2026)
Model
	Daily Request Limit (RPD)
	Tokens Per Minute (TPM)
	Requests Per Minute (RPM)

	(Note: Llama 4 Scout is currently in limited preview or paid-only on Groq for most users. Stick to Llama 3.3 70B for the best "free" quality.)

🧮 What Does This Mean for MoeAI?
You have 230 students.

230 students×10 msgs=2,300 requests/day
230 students×10 msgs=2,300 requests/day
Can Groq handle this?
✅ YES.
   * Your usage: ~2,300 req/day.
   * Groq Limit: 14,400 req/day.
   * Headroom: You are using only 16% of your free quota.
However...
If you switch to RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation), each student message might trigger 1 extra request to embed/search (if done via API) or just 1 request to the LLM.
   * Even with RAG, you are likely under 5,000 req/day.
   * Conclusion: One Groq Key is enough for your entire community of 230 students on the Free Tier.
________________


🚀 Recommended Strategy for MoeAI
Since you want the best quality without paying, here is the optimal setup:
   1. Primary Model: llama-3.3-70b-versatile
   * Why: It is significantly smarter than Llama 3.1 8B. It handles Arabic/English mixing better and solves C++/Math problems more accurately.
   * Speed: Still incredibly fast (~150 tokens/sec).
   * Cost: Free (within the 14,400 limit).
   2. Fallback Model: llama-3.1-8b-instant
   * Why: If the 70B model is temporarily rate-limited or slow, drop down to 8B for simple chats.
   3. Local Fallback (KoboldCpp):
   * Keep your RX 580 running Gemma 4 E4B or Qwen 2.5 7B.
   * Use this only if Groq goes down completely or you hit the hard cap (unlikely).

nah i dont want complex setups for the lectures thing, lets make it in agents.js
Understood. We will keep it simple and contained within agents.js.
Instead of a complex separate ingestion script or database tables, we will use a "Static Knowledge Injection" approach for now. This is perfect for your specific "DeepSeek-generated lectures" idea.
The Plan:
   1. Store Lectures as Text: You save your DeepSeek-generated lectures as simple .txt files in a folder (e.g., ~/MoeAi/backend/lectures/).
   2. Load on Startup: When server.js starts, it reads these text files into memory.
   3. Search in Memory: When a student asks a question, agents.js searches this in-memory text for keywords.
   4. Inject Context: If found, it adds the relevant lecture snippet to the AI's prompt.
This requires zero new database tables, zero external scripts, and zero complex vector math for now. It’s just good old-fashioned text search.
________________

  189  export async function agentNotify(fromAgent, toAgent, content, type = 'info') {
  190    await supabase.from('agent_messages').insert({ from_agent: fromAgent, to_agent: toAgent, content, message_type: type });
  191  }
  192  export async function getAgentInbox(agentId) {
  193    const { data } = await supabase
  194      .from('agent_messages')
  195      .select('*')
  196      .eq('to_agent', agentId)
  197      .eq('read', false)
  198      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
  199      .limit(10);
  200    if (data?.length) {
  201      await supabase.from('agent_messages').update({ read: true }).eq('to_agent', agentId).eq('read', false);
  202    }
  203    return data || [];
  204  }
  205  EOF

## 1. PROJECT OVERVIEW & GOALS


**Creator:** Moemen (18, CS Freshman, Future University in Egypt)
**Target Users:** 230+ CS students in Telegram community @CS_Epic_Save
**Core Purpose:** AI tutor that knows FUE CS curriculum, speaks English/Egyptian Arabic mix, adapts to student psychology


**Key Requirements:**
- Free/open-source only (no paid services, no bank cards)
- Runs on Moemen's hardware: ThinkPad X1 Carbon (Linux Mint) + Main PC RX 580 (Windows 10)
- Telegram bot interface (@MoeSuperBot)
- Persistent memory per student + shared institutional knowledge
- Fallback AI chain: Local KoboldCpp → Groq → Gemini
- RAG system for curriculum PDFs (3150 pages from Moodle)
- Personality: Sarcastic but supportive, 65% English / 25% Masri / 10% Franco

```sql
-- Enable UUID extension
create extension if not exists "uuid-ossp";


-- Enable pgvector for RAG
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- STUDENTS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists students (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  uni_id text unique,
  mobile text unique,
  password_hash text not null,
  full_name text not null,
  year int default 1,
  term int default 2,
  is_banned boolean default false,
  telegram_id text unique,
  daily_msg_count int default 0,
  last_msg_date text,
  is_paid boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  last_seen timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- SESSIONS (auth tokens)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists sessions (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade,
  token text unique not null,
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  expires_at timestamptz default now() + interval '30 days'
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- CONVERSATIONS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists conversations (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade,
  agent_id text not null default 'aria',
  mode text default 'standard',
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- MESSAGES
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists messages (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  conversation_id uuid references conversations(id) on delete cascade,
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade,
  role text not null check (role in ('user','agent','system')),
  content text not null,
  agent_id text default 'aria',
  mode text default 'standard',
  has_attachment boolean default false,
  attachment_urls text[],
  tokens_used int default 0,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- STUDENT MEMORY (per student, persistent)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists student_memory (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  student_id uuid references students(id) on delete cascade unique,
  weak_areas text[] default '{}',
  strong_areas text[] default '{}',
  interaction_count int default 0,
  total_messages int default 0,
  preferred_mode text default 'standard',
  psychology_notes text default '',
  last_topics text[] default '{}',
  progress_score int default 0,
  notes text default '',
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- AGENT MEMORY (each agent's growing knowledge)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists agent_memory (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  agent_id text unique not null,
  knowledge_base text default '',
  student_patterns jsonb default '{}',
  total_interactions int default 0,
  skill_extensions text default '',
  system_prompt text default '',
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- Seed agent rows
insert into agent_memory (agent_id) values 
  ('aria'),('nova'),('sentinel'),('jarvis')
on conflict (agent_id) do nothing;


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- AGENT MESSAGES (inter-agent communication)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists agent_messages (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  from_agent text not null,
  to_agent text not null,
  content text not null,
  message_type text default 'info',
  read boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- ADMIN COMMANDS (log of admin→agent interactions)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists admin_commands (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  target_agent text not null,
  command text not null,
  response text,
  broadcast boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- SYSTEM LOGS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists system_logs (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  level text default 'info' check (level in ('info','warn','error','success')),
  source text default 'server',
  message text not null,
  metadata jsonb default '{}',
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- SENTINEL INTEL (scraped data, schedules, announcements)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create table if not exists sentinel_intel (
  id uuid primary key default uuid_generate_v4(),
  source text not null,
  category text default 'general',
  title text,
  content text not null,
  url text,
  scraped_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- RAG: KNOWLEDGE CHUNKS (curriculum embeddings)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS knowledge_chunks (
  id bigserial primary key,
  content text not null,
  embedding vector(384),
  category text default 'general',
  source text,
  subject text,
  verified boolean default true,
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- RAG: INSTITUTIONAL KNOWLEDGE (profs, schedules, groups)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS institutional_knowledge (
  id bigserial primary key,
  category text not null,
  title text,
  content text not null,
  embedding vector(384),
  source text default 'manual',
  verified boolean default false,
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz default now()
);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- VECTOR SEARCH FUNCTION
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION search_knowledge(
  query_embedding vector(384),
  match_count int default 5,
  filter_subject text default null
)
RETURNS TABLE (
  id bigint,
  content text,
  category text,
  subject text,
  source text,
  similarity float
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN QUERY
  SELECT
    k.id, k.content, k.category, k.subject, k.source,
    1 - (k.embedding <=> query_embedding) as similarity
  FROM knowledge_chunks k
  WHERE
    k.embedding IS NOT NULL
    AND (filter_subject IS NULL OR k.subject = filter_subject)
  ORDER BY k.embedding <=> query_embedding
  LIMIT match_count;
END;
$$;


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- INDEXES FOR PERFORMANCE
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
create index if not exists idx_messages_conv on messages(conversation_id);
create index if not exists idx_messages_student on messages(student_id);
create index if not exists idx_messages_created on messages(created_at desc);
create index if not exists idx_conversations_student on conversations(student_id);
create index if not exists idx_sessions_token on sessions(token);
create index if not exists idx_agent_messages_to on agent_messages(to_agent, read);
create index if not exists idx_sentinel_intel_source on sentinel_intel(source, scraped_at desc);
create index if not exists idx_knowledge_embedding on knowledge_chunks USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
create index if not exists idx_institutional_embedding on institutional_knowledge USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
create index if not exists idx_knowledge_subject on knowledge_chunks(subject);


-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────
alter table students enable row level security;
alter table sessions enable row level security;
alter table conversations enable row level security;
alter table messages enable row level security;
alter table student_memory enable row level security;
-- Service role bypasses all RLS (backend uses service_role key)
```

### supabase.js Contents:
```javascript
// supabase.js — Supabase client + shared DB helpers
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';


export const supabase = createClient(
  process.env.SUPABASE_URL,
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY,
  { auth: { persistSession: false } }
);


// ─── LOGGING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function log(level, source, message, metadata = {}) {
  console.log(`[${level.toUpperCase()}] [${source}] ${message}`);
  try {
    await supabase.from('system_logs').insert({ level, source, message, metadata });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Log write failed:', e.message);
  }
}


// ─── STUDENT HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function findStudent({ uniId, mobile }) {
  if (uniId) {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('students').select('').eq('uni_id', uniId).single();
    return data;
  }
  if (mobile) {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('students').select('').eq('mobile', mobile).single();
    return data;
  }
  return null;
}


export async function createStudent({ uniId, mobile, passwordHash, fullName, year = 1, term = 2 }) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('students').insert({
    uni_id: uniId || null,
    mobile: mobile || null,
    password_hash: passwordHash,
    full_name: fullName,
    year,
    term,
  }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  // Create empty memory entry
  await supabase.from('student_memory').insert({ student_id: data.id });
  return data;
}


export async function updateLastSeen(studentId) {
  await supabase.from('students').update({ last_seen: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', studentId);
}


// ─── SESSION HELPERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function createSession(studentId, token) {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('sessions').insert({
    student_id: studentId,
    token,
  }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}


export async function validateSession(token) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('sessions')
    .select('*, students(*)')
    .eq('token', token)
    .gt('expires_at', new Date().toISOString())
    .single();
  return data;
}


export async function deleteSession(token) {
  await supabase.from('sessions').delete().eq('token', token);
}


// ─── CONVERSATION HELPERS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function getOrCreateConversation(studentId, agentId = 'aria', mode = 'standard') {
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const {  existing } = await supabase
    .from('conversations')
    .select('*')
    .eq('student_id', studentId)
    .eq('agent_id', agentId)
    .gte('created_at', today.toISOString())
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(1)
    .single();
  if (existing) return existing;
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('conversations').insert({
    student_id: studentId,
    agent_id: agentId,
    mode,
  }).select().single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data;
}


export async function getAllStudentConversations(studentId) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('conversations')
    .select('*, messages(count)')
    .eq('student_id', studentId)
    .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(20);
  return data || [];
}


// ─── ADMIN HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export async function getAllStudents() {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('students')
    .select('id, full_name, uni_id, mobile, year, term, is_banned, last_seen, created_at, student_memory(*)')
    .order('last_seen', { ascending: false });
  return data || [];
}


export async function getStudentMessages(studentId, limit = 100) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('messages')
    .select('*')
    .eq('student_id', studentId)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(limit);
  return (data || []).reverse();
}


export async function getRecentMessages(limit = 50) {
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('messages')
    .select('*, students(full_name, uni_id)')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(limit);
  return data || [];
}


export async function banStudent(studentId, ban = true) {
  await supabase.from('students').update({ is_banned: ban }).eq('id', studentId);
}


export async function saveSentinelIntel(source, category, title, content, url = null) {
  await supabase.from('sentinel_intel').insert({ source, category, title, content, url });
}


export async function getLatestIntel(source = null, limit = 20) {
  let q = supabase.from('sentinel_intel').select('*').order('scraped_at', { ascending: false }).limit(limit);
  if (source) q = q.eq('source', source);
  const { data } = await q;
  return data || [];
}
```

```javascript
const app = express();

if (!process.env.SUPABASE_URL || !process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY) { console.error('FATAL: Supabase env not set'); process.exit(1); }


// ─── MIDDLEWARE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

app.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }));
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));


// Rate limiting
const limiter = rateLimit({ windowMs: 60_000, max: 60, message: { error: 'Too many requests' } });
const strictLimiter = rateLimit({ windowMs: 60_000, max: 10, message: { error: 'Too many attempts' } });
app.use('/api/', limiter);
app.use('/api/auth/', strictLimiter);


// File uploads (memory only, no disk)

// ─── AUTH MIDDLEWARE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function requireStudent(req, res, next) {
  const token = req.headers.authorization?.replace('Bearer ', '');
  if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'No token' });
  const session = await validateSession(token);
  if (!session) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid or expired session' });
  if (session.students?.is_banned) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Account suspended' });
  req.student = session.students;
  req.studentId = session.student_id;
  updateLastSeen(req.studentId);
  next();
}


function requireAdmin(req, res, next) {
  const secret = req.headers['x-admin-secret'];
  if (!secret || secret !== ADMIN_SECRET) return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' });
  next();
}


// ─── HEALTH ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
app.get('/health', (_, res) => res.json({ status: 'ok', agents: Object.keys(AGENT_LABELS), ts: Date.now() }));
```
